```text
YAOSHI_QEMU_PROGRESS phase=ssh-ready status=complete elapsed_ms=<ms>
YAOSHI_QEMU_PROGRESS phase=dashboard-ready status=complete elapsed_ms=<ms>
YAOSHI_QEMU_PROGRESS phase=target-ready-check status=complete elapsed_ms=<ms>
```

For `phase=installer-write`, `<write_task>` is one of:

```text
verify-target
prepare-disk-beginning
prepare-disk-end
copy-yaoshi-image
finalize-writes
```

`progress.log` contains the stdout progress lines plus image resolver detail lines. Image resolver detail lines are exact:

```text
YAOSHI_IMAGE_READY status=current-hit elapsed_ms=<ms>
YAOSHI_IMAGE_READY status=current-miss reason=<missing-stamp|malformed-stamp|stale-stamp|missing-image|media-check-failed> elapsed_ms=<ms>
YAOSHI_IMAGE_READY status=build-phase-start phase=<resolver_stage> elapsed_ms=<ms>
YAOSHI_IMAGE_READY status=build-phase-cache-hit phase=<cacheable_output> elapsed_ms=<ms>
YAOSHI_IMAGE_READY status=build-phase-cache-miss phase=<cacheable_output> elapsed_ms=<ms>
YAOSHI_IMAGE_READY status=build-phase-complete phase=<resolver_stage> elapsed_ms=<ms>
YAOSHI_IMAGE_READY status=publish-complete elapsed_ms=<ms>
YAOSHI_IMAGE_READY status=complete result=<current-hit|rebuilt> elapsed_ms=<ms>
```

Image resolver detail lines exist to explain stale rebuild time during development. They are not cache metadata and are not persisted outside the QEMU output root.

The direct acceptance command uses nextest's default output capture. For live local observation, the non-acceptance diagnostic invocation is exact:

```text
cargo nextest run --locked --workspace --profile qemu --no-capture
```

The diagnostic invocation displays the same `YAOSHI_QEMU_PROGRESS` lines live. It does not change the test selection, QEMU command line, validation predicates, test output files, product build resolver, or acceptance definition.

No QEMU test passes because a screenshot exists. Functional predicates, serial markers, serial screen-frame captures, QMP operation results, structural payload validation, filesystem/image parsing, process validation output, and SSH success determine pass or fail.

### 10.6 Real-machine Dashboard redeploy and capture check

`real-dashboard-check` is the single v0.0.1 real-machine Dashboard redeploy and capture path. It is a non-gating development check owned by `yaoshi-test`. It is not a product command, not a product image build, not a QEMU test, not a release acceptance substitute, not a local-completion gate, and not a runtime configuration interface.

The command sequence is exact:

```text
cargo build --locked --target x86_64-unknown-linux-musl -p yaoshi-dashboard
cargo build --locked --target x86_64-unknown-linux-musl -p yaoshi-test --bin yaoshi-real-capture
YAOSHI_REAL_HOST=<root-ssh-host> YAOSHI_REAL_SSH_KEY=<private-key-path> cargo run --locked -p yaoshi-test --bin real-dashboard-check
```

Environment variables are exact:

| Variable              | Meaning                                                                 |
| --------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `YAOSHI_REAL_HOST`    | SSH host or address for root SSH access to one installed Yaoshi target. |
| `YAOSHI_REAL_SSH_KEY` | Path to the private key used for `root@YAOSHI_REAL_HOST`.               |

The harness accepts no other environment variable, no product flag, no configuration file, and no command-line argument.

Required preflight predicates are exact:

```text
root SSH to root@YAOSHI_REAL_HOST succeeds with BatchMode=yes
remote /usr/bin/yaoshi-dashboard is a symlink to /boot/YAOSHI/DASHBOARD/YAOSHI-DASHBOARD
remote /boot is mounted from the installed YAOSHI_ESP filesystem
remote /dev/tty1 exists
remote /dev/vcs1 exists and is readable by root
remote /dev/vcsa1 exists and is readable by root
remote virtual-console grid is at least 80x24
remote yaoshi-dashboard.service exists
```

Framebuffer artifact predicates are attempted after required preflight succeeds. Framebuffer capture state is exact:

| State      | Rule                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| ---------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `captured` | `/dev/fb0` exists, is readable by root, framebuffer variable and fixed information are readable through `FBIOGET_VSCREENINFO` and `FBIOGET_FSCREENINFO`, the format is true-color with 16, 24, or 32 bits per pixel and readable red, green, and blue channels, and the observed framebuffer-to-grid cell floor satisfies the selected Dashboard Terminus profile. |
| `skipped`  | Any `captured` predicate is false. The skip reason is one of `fb0-absent`, `fb0-unreadable`, `fbioctl-unavailable`, `fb-format-unsupported`, `fb-geometry-inconsistent`, or `fb-grid-mismatch`.                                                                                                                                                                    |

Framebuffer capture state `skipped` is not a check failure. BMC, IPMI, Redfish, browser KVM, remote-console, VNC, PNG, and OCR inputs are not substitutes for `/dev/vcs1`, `/dev/vcsa1`, or `/dev/fb0`. PNG is an output artifact only when `/dev/fb0` capture succeeds and is never an input, oracle, or pass/fail substitute.

Redeploy rules are exact:

```text
remount /boot read-write
copy local target/x86_64-unknown-linux-musl/debug/yaoshi-dashboard to /boot/YAOSHI/DASHBOARD/YAOSHI-DASHBOARD.next
chmod 0755 /boot/YAOSHI/DASHBOARD/YAOSHI-DASHBOARD.next
atomically rename /boot/YAOSHI/DASHBOARD/YAOSHI-DASHBOARD.next to /boot/YAOSHI/DASHBOARD/YAOSHI-DASHBOARD
sync
restart yaoshi-dashboard.service
remount /boot read-only
```

Persistent target modification is limited to `/boot/YAOSHI/DASHBOARD/YAOSHI-DASHBOARD`. The harness copies the static musl helper executable `yaoshi-real-capture` to `/tmp/yaoshi-real-capture` and executes it during the check. The helper writes capture output under `/run/yaoshi-real-dashboard/`. `/tmp/yaoshi-real-capture` and `/run/yaoshi-real-dashboard/` are temporary helper state and are removed before success or failure is returned when SSH remains available. The harness does not rebuild the installer image, does not rebuild the installed root ext4 image, does not modify hostname, authorized keys, loader entries, kernel, initramfs, prepare scripts, first-boot scripts, package state, partition layout, or service enablement, and does not restart any service other than `yaoshi-dashboard.service`.

Settling rules are exact:

```text
wait until yaoshi-dashboard.service is active
wait until /dev/vcs1 contains Yaoshi Dashboard v0.0.1
wait until /dev/vcs1 contains Prepare, Root, SSH, Net, and Health
wait until /dev/vcs1 contains System, Compute, Memory, Storage, and Network
wait until at least one 1000 ms Dashboard refresh interval has elapsed after the first matching Dashboard frame
```

The check output root is exact:

```text
.yaoshi/check/real-dashboard/
```

At check start, the harness removes the previous output root and recreates it. Always-created output files are exact:

```text
preflight.txt
service.txt
tty1.cells.txt
tty1.attrs.bin
sources.txt
evaluation.txt
framebuffer.info
```

Framebuffer-created output files are exact when `framebuffer_capture=captured`:

```text
framebuffer.raw
framebuffer.ppm
framebuffer.png
```

Output meanings are exact:

| File               | Meaning                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `preflight.txt`    | ASCII key-value result lines for the required preflight predicates above.                                                                                                           |
| `service.txt`      | ASCII key-value result lines for service restart and active-state checks.                                                                                                           |
| `tty1.cells.txt`   | Exact tty1 ASCII cell rows read from `/dev/vcs1` after settling.                                                                                                                    |
| `tty1.attrs.bin`   | Raw tty1 attribute buffer read from `/dev/vcsa1` after settling.                                                                                                                    |
| `sources.txt`      | Reduced ASCII snapshot of dashboard source availability for CPU, memory, SMBIOS, storage, network, thermal, and Yaoshi state.                                                       |
| `evaluation.txt`   | ASCII key-value pass/fail predicates for the non-gating check.                                                                                                                      |
| `framebuffer.info` | ASCII key-value framebuffer capture state, skip reason when skipped, geometry when captured, line length, bits-per-pixel, color channel offsets, virtual-console grid, and profile. |
| `framebuffer.raw`  | Raw visible framebuffer bytes copied from `/dev/fb0` according to `framebuffer.info`; present only when framebuffer capture is `captured`.                                          |
| `framebuffer.ppm`  | Binary PPM P6 screenshot converted from `framebuffer.raw`; present only when framebuffer capture is `captured`.                                                                     |
| `framebuffer.png`  | Deterministic PNG screenshot converted from the same normalized RGB buffer used for `framebuffer.ppm`; present only when framebuffer capture is `captured`.                         |

`framebuffer.ppm` and `framebuffer.png` conversion use the framebuffer color offsets and lengths reported by `FBIOGET_VSCREENINFO`. The helper converts true-color 16-bit, 24-bit, and 32-bit framebuffers to one normalized 8-bit RGB buffer. `framebuffer.ppm` is encoded as binary PPM P6. `framebuffer.png` is encoded as one non-interlaced truecolor PNG datastream with 8-bit RGB samples, no alpha channel, no animation, no color-management chunk, no timestamp chunk, no text metadata chunk, deterministic zlib compression settings, and deterministic chunk order `IHDR`, one or more `IDAT`, `IEND`. Paletted, grayscale, planar, unsupported packed formats, unreadable stride, or inconsistent geometry set `framebuffer_capture=skipped` and write the skip reason to `framebuffer.info`.

Evaluation predicates are exact:

```text
required preflight passed
service restart passed
Dashboard header is present in tty1.cells.txt
StatusLine groups Prepare, Root, SSH, Net, and Health are present in tty1.cells.txt
System, Compute, Memory, Storage, and Network section labels are present in tty1.cells.txt
Machine row is present and contains DMI-placeholder-filtered machine identity or unavailable fields
CPU row is present and contains CPU display identity or unavailable
DIMMs row is present as either unavailable or populated DIMM count, total capacity, vendor summary, speed summary, and ECC state
Storage Root row is present and contains block usage plus inode usage
Storage Disks row is present and contains disk count, root device, root capacity, root transport, and root disk display
Storage detail units, when present, render exactly two rows and place disk display on row 1, serial and filesystem usage on row 2
Network Route row is present and contains selected default-route state or default unavailable
Network NICs row is present and contains interface count, route summary, access summary, and attention count
Network detail units, when present, render exactly two rows and never combine an address from one interface with a gateway from another interface
all tty1.cells.txt visible bytes are ASCII 0x20 through 0x7E or LF
vcsa grid is at least 80x24
framebuffer_capture is captured or skipped
when framebuffer_capture is captured, framebuffer.ppm exists and is non-empty
when framebuffer_capture is captured, framebuffer.png exists and is non-empty
when framebuffer_capture is captured, framebuffer.ppm dimensions match framebuffer.info
when framebuffer_capture is captured, framebuffer.png dimensions match framebuffer.info
when framebuffer_capture is captured, framebuffer.ppm is not a uniform single-color image
when framebuffer_capture is captured, framebuffer.png is not a uniform single-color image
no OCR predicate is used
```

The check fails on the first failed evaluation predicate except framebuffer capture skip, which is a successful `framebuffer_capture=skipped` state. The check contains no audit record, approval record, rollback record, inventory database, provenance record, attestation record, or compliance record. The output files are developer observation and validation artifacts under `.yaoshi/check/real-dashboard/` only.

### 10.7 Completion definition

A Yaoshi product image resolve is complete when this direct command succeeds in the direnv-loaded repository environment:

```text
cargo run --locked -p yaoshi --
```

The command resolves the current installer image and publishes one raw installer image named `YaoshiInstallerImage` at `.yaoshi/out/yaoshi.img`. With an unchanged current image, it exits through the current-image fast path. On cache miss, producers validate their outputs before cache index creation. On cache hit, producer bodies are skipped. The final installer object passes the miss-time final media validation before its cache index exists, and `publish-current-image` performs the no-op equality check in Section 4.8. The command does not run QEMU and does not run nextest.

A Dashboard visible-text, layout, section-composition, source-collection, or hardware-identity rendering change is locally complete when the applicable Section 10.2 required local tests pass. QEMU and `real-dashboard-check` are not part of this Dashboard local completion definition.

For Dashboard changes that affect installed Dashboard observation, `real-dashboard-check` is the recommended physical-target redeploy check after local tests. It accelerates live validation on an already-installed Yaoshi machine by SSH redeploy and service restart. Its result is developer observation and does not change local completion or release acceptance.

Yaoshi v0.0.1 release acceptance is complete when these direct commands pass in the direnv-loaded repository environment:

```text
cargo run --locked -p yaoshi -- --version
cargo run --locked -p yaoshi --
cargo nextest run --locked --workspace
cargo nextest run --locked --workspace --profile qemu
```

The final command runs exactly one live QEMU test, `qemu::flow`, selected by Section 10.3. QEMU release acceptance validates the complete installer-to-installed runtime flow and remains mandatory before release; it is not the Dashboard physical-target redeploy and capture check.

The routine development chain is exact:

```text
cargo run --locked -p yaoshi --
  first run: resolve missing outputs, store CAS objects, publish image
  second run with unchanged inputs: use current-image fast path and leave work/cache/output untouched

cargo nextest run --locked --workspace
  static/unit/parser/renderer/model/cache checks only
```

The release acceptance chain appends this exact command after the routine development chain; it is the final command in the release acceptance command set above:

```text
cargo nextest run --locked --workspace --profile qemu
  reuse current image when current
  write last-run diagnosis and validation files under .yaoshi/check/qemu/
  run one QEMU flow
```

Documentation-only changes are locally complete after source review and any applicable non-QEMU static checks. Changing only `docs/`, comments, or non-product explanatory text does not require rerunning `qemu::flow`; QEMU is required for release acceptance and for product behavior changes that need complete installer-to-installed runtime validation, not merely because documentation was edited.

When `flake.nix`, `flake.lock`, `.envrc`, or any Nix file imported by `flake.nix` changed since the last accepted state, the Nix environment validation command in Section 2.2 is also required before the direct release acceptance commands above.

No additional command is part of v0.0.1 release acceptance. `real-dashboard-check` remains outside the release acceptance command set and outside Dashboard local completion.
